### [48] [DataPrep-Bench: Benchmarking LLMs as Training Data Preparators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20465)
*Hao Liang,Qifeng Cai,Yibo Lin,Jianzhuo Du,Qifeng Xia,Sizhe Qiu,Linzhuang Sun,Meiyi Qiang,Zhaoyang Han,Xiaochen Ma,Bohan Zeng,Ruichuan An,Conghui He,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DataPrep-Bench是首个统一的基准测试，用于评估LLM驱动数据准备的两种核心能力：数据构造（将原始来源转换为监督训练数据）和数据质量评估（预测候选数据集的训练价值）。该基准在六个领域和多个基础模型上，通过下游任务表现来共同评估这两种能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏统一的基准来测量LLM、智能体和数据驱动工作流在端到端准备训练数据方面的实际能力。数据质量评估通常关注表面文本属性而非下游训练效用，导致评估方法不一致且缺乏实际指导价值。

Method: DataPrep-Bench采用共享下游任务导向的协议进行评估。数据构造轨道中，方法消耗相同的原始来源，通过在Dolly-15k数据上进行联合微调来评分；数据质量评估轨道中，评分函数通过Pearson相关性与下游性能进行评估，引入分布对齐分数（DAS），使用候选数据集与领域代理之间的MMD来衡量分布对齐度。

Result: 在数据构造方面，发布的数据构造智能体在Llama-3.1-8B Finance任务上比Dolly基线提升近20个百分点；在数据质量评估方面，DAS在四个领域达到最强跨模型相关性，是唯一同时在数学、科学和医学领域达到r＞0.70的指标，优于现有基于质量、多样性和启发式的评估器。

Conclusion: DataPrep-Bench提供了一个统一的、下游任务导向的框架，将数据构造和数据质量评估视为LLM驱动数据准备的平等目标，为测量两种能力的进步提供了标准化的评估环境。

Abstract: The quality of training data fundamentally determines the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), yet no unified benchmark exists to measure how well LLMs, agents, and data-centric workflows actually prepare training data end to end. We view LLM-driven data preparation as comprising two complementary capabilities: data construction, which transforms raw sources into supervised training data, and data quality evaluation, which predicts the training value of candidate datasets before downstream training; throughout, "quality" refers to downstream training utility rather than surface-level textual properties. We introduce DataPrep-Bench, the first unified benchmark that jointly evaluates both capabilities under a shared downstream-grounded protocol over six domains and multiple base models. For data construction, methods consume identical raw sources and are scored by fine-tuning a base model on their outputs jointly with Dolly-15k; alongside this track we release Data-Construction-Skill, a skill-guided agent that lifts the Dolly-only baseline by nearly 20 points absolute on Llama-3.1-8B Finance and is competitive with the strongest agent- and DataFlow-based methods in knowledge-extraction-dense domains. For data quality evaluation, scoring functions are scored by Pearson correlation with downstream performance on a shared candidate pool; we release the Distributional Alignment Score (DAS), a distribution-based evaluator that uses MMD between a candidate dataset and a domain proxy. DAS attains the strongest cross-model correlation in four of six domains and is the only metric clearing r > 0.70 simultaneously in Math, Science, and Medical, outperforming existing quality-, diversity-, and heuristic-based evaluators. DataPrep-Bench provides a unified, downstream-grounded framework for measuring progress on both capabilities as co-equal targets of LLM-driven data preparation.

</details>


### [49] [Scaling Closed-Loop Feature Channel Configuration with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20516)
*Tolgay Atinc Uzun,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究验证了LLM驱动的神经网络通道配置搜索在更大采样规模下的可扩展性，证实了优化信号能转移至密集采样，并发现了非2的幂次通道宽度等架构规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期研究表明LLM驱动的通道配置搜索有前景，但评估样本稀疏，作者希望验证优化行为在更密集采样下的可转移性，并观察是否出现新的架构规律。

Method: 将原搜索设置扩展为每轮256个候选网络（原文误为250），进行8轮完整搜索共生成2000个候选，经任务和元数据过滤后得到462个CIFAR-100验证结果。

Result: 每轮平均准确率呈正线性趋势（斜率9.87e-4，p=0.043），前沿性能显著提升（最佳准确率从0.3144增至0.3676，参数量从166.5M降至11.8M）。大样本揭示41.8%验证候选采用非2的幂次通道宽度，强模型呈现中等早期宽度、后续扩展的结构化分配模式。

Conclusion: 初始研究观察到的通道搜索信号在密集采样下有效转移，且更大样本暴露出新的架构规律，表明LLM驱动搜索具有可扩展性和架构发现潜力。

Abstract: Promising initial results in closed-loop large-language-model-based channel-configuration search demonstrated that neural-network widths can be optimized directly through executable code generation and accuracy feedback. However, those results were obtained from a relatively sparse set of valid evaluations, leaving open whether the observed optimization behavior transfers to a denser sampling regime and whether additional architectural regularities emerge when more generated networks are evaluated. To test this, the same search setting is scaled to 250 candidate networks per fine-tuning cycle. The analysis covers 2000 generated candidates from 8 complete cycles, yielding 462 verified CIFAR-100 evaluations after task and metadata filtering. Per-cycle mean accuracy exhibits a positive linear trend with slope 9.87e-4 (p=0.043), while the high-performing frontier improves more strongly: the best observed accuracy increases from 0.3144 to 0.3676, and both the top-5 and top-10 cycle-level means exhibit positive trends. The scaled run also reveals improved parameter efficiency. The best model reaches 0.3676 with 11.8M parameters, compared with an early high-performing model at 0.3144 with 166.5M parameters. Beyond accuracy, the larger sample exposes architectural regularities that were difficult to assess from sparse observations. Non-power-of-two channel widths occur in 41.8% of verified candidates, and the strongest models share structured channel-allocation patterns characterized by moderate early widths and expanded middle or later blocks. These findings indicate that the channel-search signal observed in the initial study transfers

</details>


### [50] [Multimodal CoLRAG-TF: Triple-Filtered Retrieval for Complex PDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20517)
*Takato Yasuno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Multimodal CoLRAG-TF proposes a four-axis fusion architecture combining dense embeddings, BM25, knowledge-graph triples, and image similarity to enable robust retrieval over heterogeneous PDFs, achieving 0.9909 retrieval recall and 71.6% multi-hop improvement.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Retrieval-augmented generation over heterogeneous PDF collections faces challenges including multimodal content, domain-specific terminology, and the need for multi-hop reasoning across dispersed evidence.

Method: A four-axis fusion architecture integrating dense text embeddings, BM25 keyword matching, knowledge-graph triple filtering, and image-based similarity. The system uses a hybrid OCR pipeline and LLM-based caption generation to construct a multimodal index from 2,403 blocks in 43 Japanese disaster lesson PDFs, extracting 11,414 OpenIE triples indexed with FAISS. A HippoRAG2-inspired coarse-to-fine retriever (volume→chapter→block) narrows the search space, with Bayesian optimization determining optimal fusion weights.

Result: Bayesian optimization revealed the triple axis must dominate (α_triple = 0.44) to counteract lexical bias. Evaluated on a 457-pair benchmark, the system achieved a Retrieval Recall of 0.9909 and 71.6% improvement in multi-hop answer similarity over single-hop queries. An image-to-lesson pipeline with vision LLM demonstrated applicability to visual inputs.

Conclusion: Triple-filtered multimodal fusion is essential for structured reasoning over noisy, heterogeneous PDFs and provides a general framework applicable beyond the disaster domain.

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over heterogeneous PDF collections remains challenging due to multimodal content, domain-specific terminology, and the need for multi-hop reasoning across dispersed evidence. We present Multimodal CoLRAG-TF, a four-axis fusion architecture that integrates dense text embeddings, BM25 keyword matching, knowledge-graph triple filtering, and image-based similarity for robust retrieval over complex documents. Our system constructs a multimodal index of 2,403 blocks extracted from 43 Japanese disaster lesson PDFs, supported by a hybrid OCR pipeline and LLM-based caption generation. To enhance compositional reasoning, we extract 11,414 OpenIE triples and index them with FAISS, enabling sub-second triple lookup and hierarchical propagation of relevance signals. A HippoRAG2-inspired coarse-to-fine retriever (volume $\to$ chapter $\to$ block) narrows the search space before final fusion scoring. Bayesian optimization over fusion weights reveals that the triple axis must dominate ($α_\text{triple} = 0.44$) to counteract lexical bias and sustain multi-hop retrieval quality. Evaluated on a 457-pair benchmark, Multimodal CoLRAG-TF achieves a Retrieval Recall of 0.9909 and a 71.6$\%$ improvement in multi-hop answer similarity over single-hop queries. An image-to-lesson pipeline using a vision LLM further demonstrates the applicability of the approach to visual inputs. These results show that triple-filtered multimodal fusion is essential for structured reasoning over noisy, heterogeneous PDFs and provides a general framework applicable beyond the disaster domain.

</details>


### [51] [Uncertainty-Aware Trust Estimation for Multi-LLM Systems via Structured Expert Judgement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20529)
*Jiawei Zheng,Jiazhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种不确定性感知的信任估计方法来改进多LLM集成，通过Cooke风格的对数权重校准专家可靠性，在异构和污染专家面板上表现优于简单聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM聚合方法假设所有模型同等可信，忽视了模型间不确定性质量差异；异构LLM的可靠性和能力差异显著，简单聚合容易受到不可靠或对抗性专家影响。

Method: 将多LLM聚合视为不确定性感知的信任估计问题，采用决策论中的结构化专家判断，通过上下文感知校准问题基于概率预测质量估计专家可靠性，使用Cooke风格对数权重惩罚过度自信错误预测，奖励校准良好的专家。

Result: 在MMLU和MMLU-Pro上评估，在均匀设置中聚合方法表现相似，但在异构和污染情况下Cooke权重至关重要，在引入不可靠专家时仍保持稳健，实现更优的准确率-可靠性平衡。

Conclusion: 多LLM聚合不仅需要组合预测，还需要在不确定性下校准信任；Cooke权重方法在处理异构和污染专家面板时显著优于简单聚合，为LLM集成提供了更可靠的方法论。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) ensembles are increasingly used to improve reliability by combining predictions from multiple LLMs. However, existing aggregation methods typically assume that all models are equally trustworthy, overlooking differences in uncertainty quality. This assumption is poorly suited to heterogeneous LLMs, whose reliability and capability vary significantly, making naive aggregation vulnerable to unreliable or adversarial experts. In this work, we formulate multi-LLM aggregation as a problem of uncertainty-aware trust estimation. We adapt structured expert judgment from decision theory, using context-aware calibration questions to estimate expert reliability based on the quality of its probabilistic predictions. Specifically, we employ Cooke-style log weighting, which penalises overconfident incorrect predictions and favours well-calibrated experts. We evaluate our approach on MMLU and MMLU-Pro across homogeneous, heterogeneous, and contaminated expert panels. Results show that while aggregation methods perform similarly in homogeneous settings, Cooke weighting becomes critical under heterogeneity and contamination. It achieves a superior accuracy-reliability balance and remains robust when unreliable experts are introduced. These findings suggest that Multi-LLM aggregation requires not just combining predictions, but calibrating trust under uncertainty.

</details>


### [52] [Position: Stop Reactively Patching Your Model Every Time and Start Proactive Test-Driven AI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20532)
*Nadine Chang,Maying Shen,Jialiang Wang,Rafid Mahmood,Jose M. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出采用“主动测试驱动飞轮”替代传统的“反应式AI飞轮”，通过构建“测试空间”将反馈数据映射到任务目标，以解决反应式飞轮忽视上下文、无法预判边缘案例、迭代次数多以及长尾数据收集困难等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统在多样化和开放式的用例中运行，当前维护管道多采用反应式AI飞轮——仅基于用户错误反馈进行修补。这种方法存在三大局限：1）忽视错误在系统目标中的更广泛上下文；2）无法预判潜在的边缘案例，导致更多不必要的飞轮迭代；3）由于开放世界用例的长尾特性，收集剩余错误在统计上越来越困难。

Method: 本文倡导创建“测试空间”，在技术上将反馈数据映射到任务目标，使飞轮从反应式转变为主动式。通过数学证明，主动测试驱动飞轮能够在更少的迭代次数下实现更好的长期扩展性。

Result: 理论证明表明，主动测试驱动飞轮比反应式飞轮具有更优的长期扩展能力，能够在更少的迭代次数内达到更好的性能。

Conclusion: 为了构建更具泛化能力的系统，AI维护管道需要从反应式转向主动测试驱动模式，通过测试空间的设计实现从被动修补到主动预判的转变。

Abstract: Many modern AI systems are designed to operate under diverse, open-ended, use-cases. To help generalize deployed systems, many deployed-system maintenance pipelines use a reactive AI flywheel that observes emerging feedback from user behavior (errors) and patches the model accordingly. However, when used as the primary maintenance mechanism, these flywheels often ignore the broader context of these errors within the system's objectives, failing to preempt potential future edge cases, which leads to more unnecessary flywheel iterations. Also, it is statistically increasingly difficult to collect remaining errors due to the long-tail nature of open-world use-cases. This position paper argues that a proactive test-driven flywheel is required to address reactive flywheel's limitations and to approach a generalizable system. We advocate for creating a "test space" to technically map feedback data to task objectives, evolving the flywheel from reactive to proactive. We augment our position by mathematically proving a proactive one achieves better long-term scaling with fewer iterations than the reactive flywheel.

</details>


### [53] [StabilityBench: Benchmarking Instability in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20558)
*Emma Kondrup,Zachary Yang,Anne Imouza,Reihaneh Rabbany*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StabilityBench 提出了一种将单轮基准查询转换为多轮对话历史的基准操作符，通过注入现实用户模拟（如人口统计代理或阿谀奉承诱饵）来测试大语言模型在真实场景中的稳定性，结果证明模型性能在多轮交互中显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI助手在医疗、政府等高风险场景中广泛应用，但当前评估协议仍以静态、单轮测试为主，难以捕捉对话中的上下文依赖性和真实世界的变异性。

Method: StabilityBench 是一个原则性、通用且模型无关的基准操作符，通过将单轮查询转化为多轮交互历史，并注入人口统计代理或阿谀奉承诱饵等真实用户模拟来增强现有基准，同时保持原始任务意图；还提出了保持规模不变的 StabilityBench-Mini 变体。

Result: 在数学推理、健康问答和安全四个基准上测试了九个大语言模型，发现模型在这些注入条件下性能普遍不稳定，其中三个基准的性能出现显著下降。

Conclusion: 静态评估存在重要局限性，需要更接近真实场景的评估设置，StabilityBench 提供了实现这一目标的新框架。

Abstract: AI Assistants are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, such as healthcare or government services. Yet their real-world behavior remains poorly understood due to strong context dependence. Current evaluation protocols follow a defense-in-depth paradigm with compounding layers of safeguards, ranging from traditional benchmarks to live or adversarial testing. Such benchmarks remain largely static and single-turn, limiting their ability to capture real-world variability in conversational settings. We propose StabilityBench, a principled, general and model-agnostic benchmark operator that transforms single-turn benchmark queries into multi-turn interaction histories. StabilityBench augments existing benchmarks by injecting realistic user simulations, through demographic proxies or sycophantic baits, while preserving original task intent. We apply StabilityBench to four benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning, health question-answering and safety, and evaluate nine large language models under these conditions. Our results show that model performance is consistently unstable under these injections, with considerable performance degradations on three out of four benchmarks studied. These highlight important limitations of static evaluations and motivate more realistic evaluation settings. To this end, we propose StabilityBench-Mini: a size-preserving variant of StabilityBench that samples across diversification axes, enabling more realistic evaluation without increasing costs.

</details>


### [54] [Chronofy: A Temporal-Logical Decay Architecture for Information Validity in Time-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20560)
*Muntaser Syed,Marius Silaghi,Sheikh Abujar,Sharun Akter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chronofy是一个三层神经符号框架，通过在RAG系统中嵌入时间衰减机制来防止时间幻觉。它将事实年龄结构化地嵌入表示中，在检索层引入可学习的指数衰减函数，并在推理层应用信号时序逻辑(STL)来评估时间有效性，从而提升检索精度并减少时间幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG系统将所有检索到的事实视为同等有效，忽略了时间来源，导致时间幻觉（过时事实污染输出）。临床例子表明时间相关性很重要：昨天的实验室读数是可操作的，而六个月前的读数则是噪声。

Method: 三层架构实现时间-逻辑衰减架构(TLDA)：Layer 1在Matryoshka嵌入中预留专用时间子空间，使事实年龄在表示中不可移除；Layer 2在基于图的检索中集成可学习指数衰减函数，衰减系数β_j基于贝叶斯决策理论（近似两倍潜在过程均值回归率）；Layer 3应用信号时序逻辑(STL)鲁棒性函数评估检索知识的时间有效性，并执行可能性最弱链原则。

Result: 在时间知识图谱预测基准、TimE时间问答基准和领域特定敏感性分析上评估，显示显式时间衰减建模提高了检索精度，减少了时间幻觉，并在时间上下文不足时实现了原则性数据重新获取触发。

Conclusion: 显式时间衰减建模能够提升检索精度、减少时间幻觉，并在时间上下文不足时提供原则性数据重新获取机制。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems retrieve and integrate external knowledge to ground large language model (LLM) outputs. However, current RAG architectures treat all retrieved facts as equally valid regardless of temporal provenance, leading to temporal hallucination, where plausible but obsolete facts corrupt the output. A clinical lab reading from yesterday is actionable; the same reading from six months ago is noise. We present Chronofy, a three-layer neuro-symbolic framework implementing the Temporal-Logical Decay Architecture (TLDA) that embeds temporal validity directly into the representation, retrieval, and reasoning layers of RAG systems. Layer 1 reserves a dedicated temporal subspace within Matryoshka embeddings to make fact age structurally irremovable from the representation. Layer 2 integrates learnable exponential decay functions into graph-based retrieval, where the decay coefficient $β_j$ is grounded in Bayesian decision theory as an approximation of twice the latent process mean-reversion rate. Layer 3 applies Signal Temporal Logic (STL) robustness functions to evaluate the temporal validity of retrieved knowledge, not LLM output confidence, and enforces the possibilistic weakest-link principle to bound output confidence by the most decayed evidence in the reasoning chain. We evaluate Chronofy on temporal knowledge graph forecasting benchmarks, the TimE temporal QA benchmark, and a domain-specific sensitivity analysis, demonstrating that explicit temporal decay modeling improves retrieval precision, reduces temporal hallucination, and enables principled data re-acquisition triggers when temporal context is insufficient.

</details>


### [55] [Are Single-Token Sparse Autoencoder Features Causally Necessary? Layer-Depth and SAE-Family Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20596)
*Seonglae Cho,Zekun Wu,Kleyton Da Costa,Rishi Kalra,Ilham Wicaksono,Adriano Koshiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究分析了稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征在不同模型和SAE家族中的因果作用稳定性，发现跨家族因果差异显著，训练方法比激活函数或规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE特征被广泛用于解释和引导大型语言模型，但不同SAE家族中特征的因果作用是否稳定尚不清楚。单token特征提供了诊断案例，因为其ground truth允许直接比较。

Method: 分析390万个特征，涵盖6个模型和3个SAE家族，使用零消融在完整层深度进行。评估单token特征在解码器空间中的聚类紧密度、层分布、消融效果以及跨家族差异。

Result: 单token特征在解码器空间中聚类更紧密（4.7倍），集中在早期层。消融这些特征在178/208个全层条件下显著降低logits。跨家族因果差异超过家族内部规模效应。目标token排名在96-98%的情况下恢复到基线的2倍以内。控制激活函数比较显示训练配方是剩余候选因素。

Conclusion: 跨家族可解释性声明对训练方法敏感，而不仅仅是激活函数或规模。不同SAE家族（GemmaScope、BatchTopK、LlamaScope）的特征因果作用存在显著差异。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoder (SAE) features are used to interpret and steer large language models, yet whether a feature's causal role is stable across SAE families remains untested. Single-token features that activate on one vocabulary item provide the diagnostic case where ground truth permits direct comparison. We analyze 3.9M features across six models and three SAE families using zero-ablation at full layer depth. Single-token features cluster 4.7x tighter in decoder space and concentrate in early layers (Layer 0 in GPT2-Small; L0-L4 in Gemma). Ablating them yields Benjamini-Hochberg-significant logit reductions in 178 of 208 full-layer conditions, with depth controlling whether damage cascades downstream or shapes the output directly. Cross-family causal differences exceed within-family scale effects: on the same base model, GemmaScope and BatchTopK features remain causally anchored, while LlamaScope features are locally redundant. The target token's rank recovers to within 2x baseline 96-98% of the time after the same ablation, and a controlled activation-function comparison reverses sign within the same model, leaving training recipe as the residual candidate. Cross-family interpretability claims are therefore sensitive to training methodology, not just activation function or scale.

</details>


### [56] [SalesLoop: Reinforcement Learning from Performance Feedback for Sales Lead Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20655)
*Chenyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SalesLoop是针对CRM系统销售线索排名问题的强化学习框架，通过闭环反馈机制解决离线与在线性能不匹配、目标函数不一致和时序分布漂移三大问题，在离线和生产环境中均实现了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRM系统中的线索排名模型在离线评估中表现优异，但在生产环境中往往表现不佳。作者识别了导致这种性能脱节的三大根本原因：1) 离线与在线评估指标的不匹配；2) 点对点与列表式优化目标的错位；3) 时序分布漂移。

Method: SalesLoop采用强化学习框架，建立模型预测与真实商业结果之间的闭环反馈：1) 引入性能感知奖励函数，对排名位置和转化速度加权的转化结果进行编码；2) 提出Discriminative GRPO（分组相对策略优化），将GRPO适应于判别式排序模型，实现列表式优化。

Result: 离线评估显示，相比最强静态基线，SalesLoop使NDCG@K提升7.9%，P@K提升15.8%。在一家新能源汽车制造商的160天生产A/B测试中（涉及1650万线索和280名销售专员），实现了统计显著的正向提升：累计提升4.7%（p=0.047）和8.7%（p=0.002）。生产环境中，排名模型的Top-10%召回率达到44.1%，高意向线索的转化率是专家基线的2.3倍。

Conclusion: SalesLoop通过强化学习闭环反馈机制有效解决了CRM线索排名中的离线-在线性能差距问题，在离线和生产环境中均取得了显著且 statistically significant 的性能提升，证明了强化学习在商业应用中的价值。

Abstract: Lead ranking in Customer Relationship Management (CRM) systems faces a persistent challenge: models achieving high offline accuracy often underperform in production. We identify three fundamental gaps responsible for this disconnect: offline-online metric mismatch, pointwise-listwise objective misalignment, and temporal distribution drift. To address these gaps, we propose SalesLoop, a reinforcement learning framework that establishes a closed feedback loop between model predictions and real-world business outcomes. Our approach introduces (1) a performance-aware reward that encodes conversion outcomes weighted by ranking position and conversion velocity, and (2) Discriminative GRPO, a listwise optimization objective that adapts Group Relative Policy Optimization to discriminative ranking models.
  SalesLoop improves NDCG@K by +7.9\% and P@K by +15.8\% over the strongest static baseline. A 160-day production A/B test at a New Energy Vehicle manufacturer, spanning 16.5M leads and 280 sales specialists across two provincial markets, validates statistically significant cumulative lift of +4.7\% ($p=0.047$) and +8.7\% ($p=0.002$). In production, the ranking backbone achieves Top-10\% recall of 44.1\% and surfaces high-intent leads at $2.3\times$ the conversion rate of specialist baselines.

</details>


### [57] [Offline RL with Hierarchical Action Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20834)
*Ahad Jawaid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiQC 是一种分层离线目标条件强化学习算法，通过将高层潜在规划与低层动作分块相结合，解决了长期任务中的“视界诅咒”问题，在 OGBench 基准测试中取得了最佳性能，特别是在人形巨物导航等长期任务上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习虽然可以从静态数据集中学习通用策略，但在扩展到长期任务时面临“视界诅咒”挑战，即价值估计误差会通过长链的引导贝尔曼备份而累积。现有的分层方法虽然通过分解任务为子目标来缓解问题，但通常依赖存在短视执行和有偏价值估计的低层控制器。

Method: HiQC 结合高层潜在规划与低层动作分块，让低层批评器在时间扩展的动作序列上条件化，从而实现无偏的 k 步价值备份，在规划和执行层面同时压缩视界。理论分析表明，这种双重分解在给定每次备份误差模型下比标准分层或扁平分块能得到更紧的价值误差界限。

Result: 理论证明 HiQC 的双重分解在固定每次备份误差模型下比标准分层或扁平分块提供更紧的价值误差界限。实验上，HiQC 在 OGBench 基准测试中表现出最高的综合性能，在人形巨物导航等长期导航任务上取得最大增益。

Conclusion: HiQC 通过在规划和执行层面同时压缩视界，有效缓解了离线强化学习中的视界诅咒，在理论保证和实证结果上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (RL) holds the promise of learning general-purpose policies from static datasets. However, scaling these methods to long-horizon tasks remains a challenge due to the curse of horizon, where value estimation errors can compound through long chains of bootstrapped Bellman backups. Existing hierarchical approaches mitigate this by decomposing tasks into subgoals, yet they often rely on low-level controllers that suffer from myopic execution and biased value estimates. In this work, we propose Hierarchical Implicit Q-Chunking (HiQC), an offline goal-conditioned RL algorithm that combines high-level latent planning with low-level action chunking. By conditioning the low-level critic on temporally extended action sequences, HiQC enables unbiased k-step value backups, compressing the horizon at both the planning and execution levels. We theoretically demonstrate that this dual decomposition results in a tighter bound on value error under a bounded per-backup error model compared to standard hierarchy or flat chunking alone. Empirically, HiQC achieves the highest aggregate performance among the compared methods on the OGBench suite, with its largest gains on long-horizon navigation tasks such as humanoid-giant.

</details>


### [58] [Multi-turn RL with Structural and Performance Aware Rewards for CUDA Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20908)
*Quazi Ishtiaque Mahmud,Nesreen K. Ahmed,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CudaPerf是一种强化学习框架，通过结合可验证执行奖励和基于并行化特征的结构代码感知奖励，优化CUDA代码生成，在C到CUDA和PyTorch到CUDA转换中显著优于现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法仅依赖正确性和加速比等结果信号，忽略了优化代码所需的关键程序结构性能属性（如内存合并、占用率、算术强度和同步模式）。

Method: CudaPerf采用两阶段方法：(1)离线成对排序模块通过对比学习区分强/弱程序候选；(2)在线RL训练阶段通过统一奖励信号联合优化正确性、性能和结构效率；采用迭代细化机制；包含2.9k C到CUDA和1k PyTorch到CUDA程序的标注数据集。

Result: 在C到CUDA转换中，CudaPerf相比Qwen-3-32B实现5倍加速比和17%正确率提升；在PyTorch到CUDA转换中，相比CUDA Agent实现3.32倍加速比和7%正确率提升。

Conclusion: CudaPerf显著超越强基线模型，通过整合执行反馈和结构感知奖励，实现了程序优化生成能力的全面提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful technique to enhance the reasoning capacity of LLMs for optimized code generation. However, existing RLVR approaches primarily rely on outcome-based signals such as correctness and speedup, overlooking performance-critical structural properties of programs that are essential for generating optimized code. In this work, we propose CudaPerf, a reflective RL framework that incorporates both verifiable execution rewards and structural code-aware rewards derived from parallelization features (e.g., memory coalescing, occupancy, Arithmatic Intensity, and synchronization patterns). CudaPerf operates in two stages: (1) an offline pairwise ranking module that learns to distinguish strong and weak program candidates via contrastive comparisons, and (2) an online RL training phase that jointly optimizes for correctness, performance, and structural efficiency through a unified reward signal. To further enhance learning, CudaPerf utilizes iterative refinement using execution feedback enabling progressive improvement of generated candidates. We also introduce a dataset comprising 2.9k C to CUDA and 1k PyTorch to CUDA programs, each paired with diverse input configurations and multiple CUDA implementations encompassing diverse optimization strategies. CudaPerf is evaluated across multiple benchmarks comprising both C to CUDA and PyTorch to CUDA transformations. Empirical findings suggest that CudaPerf significantly outperforms strong baselines, including Qwen-3-32B (for C to CUDA) and CUDA Agent (for PyTorch to CUDA) by achieving up to 5X & 3.32X improvements in speedup, and 17% & 7% improvements in correctness, respectively.

</details>


### [59] [Nipping the Butterfly Effect in the Bud: Self-Output Fine-Tuning for Autoregressive Weather Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21080)
*Yun-Ye Cai,Hsuan-Tien Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对自回归深度学习天气预测（DLWP）在长时间尺度预测中误差快速累积的问题，提出了一种名为“自输出微调（SOFT）”的即插即用策略，通过利用模型自身的一步预测结果来校正首个时间步出现的有偏输入分布，从而显著提升长时间天气预报的准确性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时间天气预报是大气科学的核心挑战之一。尽管自回归深度学习天气预测（DLWP）具有高度可扩展性和灵活性，但其预测误差在长时间尺度上会迅速累积。作者认为这一现象源于输出误差与输入分布偏移之间的反馈循环，即早期的小误差会通过自回归过程被放大，进而破坏后续输入分布，最终导致预测精度随预测时长的增加而急剧下降。

Method: 本研究从理论和实证两个角度分析了误差增长机制，揭示了其源于输出误差与输入分布偏移之间的反馈循环。针对这一问题，作者提出了“自输出微调（Self-Output Fine-Tuning, SOFT）”策略：这是一种即插即用的方法，通过利用模型自身的一步预测结果来校正首个时间步出现的有偏输入分布，从而在自回归过程中保持输入分布的稳定性。

Result: 实验结果表明，尽管SOFT策略简单直接，但它在长时间天气预报任务上取得了最先进的性能，显著降低了预测误差和分布差异。该研究还发现输入分布的偏移早在自回归过程的第一个时间步就已出现，且可以通过检测分布外特征来识别。

Conclusion: SOFT的成功强调了重新审视深度学习天气预测基本流程的重要性，代表了大科学领域在管道改进方面的一项关键进展。该研究为缓解自回归模型在长时间尺度上的误差累积问题提供了新的思路和有效工具。

Abstract: Long-horizon weather forecasting is a fundamental challenge in atmospheric science, for which autoregressive Deep Learning Weather Prediction (DLWP) has emerged as the primary paradigm. Although the autoregressive pipeline is highly scalable and flexible, its prediction errors grow rapidly over long forecasting horizons. In this work, we study this error growth phenomenon from both theoretical and empirical perspectives. Our analysis reveals that the growth is driven by a feedback loop between output errors and input distribution shifts. Specifically, the autoregressive process amplifies small initial output errors, which progressively corrupt subsequent input distributions, echoing the butterfly effect in atmospheric science and ultimately deteriorating forecasting accuracy over longer horizons. Furthermore, we show that this distributional shift originates at the earliest stage of inference, with out-of-distribution signatures detectable as early as the first autoregressive step. To mitigate this issue, we propose \textbf{Self-Output Fine-Tuning (SOFT)}, a plug-and-play strategy that leverages the model's own one-step predictions to calibrate the biased input distribution encountered at the first step. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, despite its simplicity, SOFT achieves state-of-the-art performance on long-horizon forecasting tasks and substantially reduces both prediction errors and distributional discrepancy. The success of SOFT highlights the importance of reexamining the fundamental pipeline of deep learning weather prediction, representing a critical pipeline advance for atmospheric science.

</details>


### [60] [Training Large Language Models for Self-Explanation Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21090)
*Yeoktatt Cheah,María Pérez-Ortiz,Noah Y. Siegel,Oana-Maria Camburu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种强化学习方法直接优化大语言模型自解释的可信度，将可信度指标转化为训练目标，在多个模型上显著提升了自解释的可信度，并验证了模型能隐式识别并披露影响决策的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅评估或通过推理时提示改进自解释的可信度，但缺乏直接优化模型参数以生成可信自解释的机制。研究旨在填补这一空白，探索RL是否能直接优化模型披露影响决策的因素，从而提高LLM自解释的可信度。

Method: 将现有的可信度指标（Phi-CCT相关性）转化为强化学习的训练目标，采用两种干预类型（随机词插入和用户偏差插入）作为输入扰动，使用Phi-CCT相关性作为样本级奖励函数。在Llama3.1-8B和Qwen3-8B上通过RL微调，并在StrategyQA等任务上进行评估。

Result: RL微调后的模型在可信度指标上显著提升：分布内分数从接近0升至最高0.664，分布外分数在StrategyQA上达0.691。跨干预泛化显示出一定迁移能力（如Llama3.1-8B在仅随机词插入训练后对用户偏差短语也表现出非零迁移）。分析表明模型未出现奖励作弊行为。

Conclusion: 模型可以通过RL训练隐式识别影响决策的关键因素并披露它们，为降低LLM不可信推理提供了一条可扩展路径。这种方法证明了直接参数优化能显著提升自解释的可信度，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We propose a Reinforcement Learning (RL) method to directly optimize the faithfulness of self-explanations - the extent to which a model's generated reasoning accurately reflects its internal decision-making process. While existing work focuses on evaluating faithfulness or using inference-time prompting frameworks to improve an LLM's self-explanation's tractability, these approaches do not provide a mechanism to directly optimize a model's parameters to generate faithful self-explanations. We bridge this gap by modifying existing faithfulness metrics into an RL training objective. We investigate (1) if models can be trained to accurately detect factors that affect their decisions, and (2) whether RL can directly optimize for the disclosure of these factors thereby improving LLM self-explanations' faithfulness. We experiment with two intervention types: random-word insertions and user-bias insertions, using a per-sample reward derived from the Phi-CCT correlation metric. RL fine-tuned Llama3.1-8B and Qwen3-8B show substantial improvements on the Phi-CCT faithfulness metric, with in-distribution scores rising from near-zero to as high as 0.664, and out-of-distribution scores reaching up to 0.691 on held-out tasks such as StrategyQA. Cross-intervention generalization is weaker but more interesting: a priori we would not expect a model trained only on random word insertions to generalize to user-bias phrases, yet Llama3.1-8B shows non-zero transfer in this direction. The reverse direction and Qwen3-8B do not replicate this, indicating model-dependent and setup-dependent effects we cannot yet explain. Lastly we analyze model behavior to rule out reward gaming behaviors that often plague RL training. Ultimately, we show that models can be trained to implicitly identify influential factors and disclose them, offering a scalable path toward reducing unfaithful reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


### [61] [Automated Synthesis and Adversarial Validation of Executable Causal Research Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21173)
*Irena Girshovitz,Dan Zeltzer,Ran Gilad-Bachrach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了基于AI的流行病学研究助手（ARA），通过显式编码因果设计原则、研究假设和方法约束，使自动化研究系统中的“静默失败”变得可见。该框架整合了协议构建、合成数据生成和对抗性验证，将自然语言研究问题转化为结构化因果协议和可执行分析代码，并通过控制违反识别假设来评估生成分析的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化研究系统虽然能够加速实证分析，但存在严重的“静默失败”问题——即分析代码执行成功但依赖于无效的因果假设。这类失败往往在代码层面不会报错，导致研究者可能误将不正确的因果推断结果视为有效结论。因此需要一种能够显式编码因果设计原则并使这些失败可见的框架。

Method: ARA框架将协议构建、合成数据生成和对抗性验证整合到一个统一流程中。首先将自然语言研究问题转化为结构化因果协议，然后使用已知ground-truth效应的结构因果模型（SCMs）生成合成数据集。最后在控制违反识别假设的情况下评估生成的分析。该框架特别适用于访问受限数据（如医疗数据）时的管道开发。

Result: 在自动化因果推理基准测试中评估显示：协议构建和对抗性验证并未比标准LLM生成方法一致性地提高与基准估计的数值一致性。然而，它们改变了失败模式——ARA不再静默地返回因果估计，而是经常暴露协议问题、诊断失败、推断不完整或降级非因果解释。这表明该框架能够识别何时因果主张是缺乏依据的。

Conclusion: 有效性优先的自动化科学系统应不仅通过答案准确性来评估，还应评估其是否能够指示何时因果主张是不恰当的。这种方法确保了自动化研究工具在追求效率的同时，不会掩盖潜在的因果推断错误，从而提高了科研工作的可信度和可靠性。

Abstract: While automated research systems promise to accelerate empirical analysis, they are prone to silent failures: instances in which analysis code executes successfully yet relies on invalid causal assumptions. We present the Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based Epidemiology Research Assistant (ARA), a framework that makes these failures visible by explicitly encoding causal design principles, study-specific assumptions, and methodological constraints. ARA integrates protocol construction, synthetic data generation, and adversarial validation into a unified pipeline. The framework translates natural language research questions into structured causal protocols and executable analysis code by first constructing a protocol and then generating synthetic datasets using Structural Causal Models (SCMs) with known ground-truth effects. This synthetic-data step can also support pipeline development when access to confidential data, such as medical data, is restricted. The generated analysis is then evaluated under controlled violations of identification assumptions. We evaluate ARA on the Automated Causal Reasoning Benchmark, assessing recovery of identification strategies, causal quantities, treatment and outcome variables, and consistency between generated code and approved protocol. Protocol construction and adversarial validation did not consistently improve numerical agreement with benchmark estimates compared with standard LLM-based generation. However, they changed the failure mode: instead of silently returning causal estimates, ARA often surfaced protocol concerns, diagnostic failures, incomplete inference, or downgraded non-causal interpretations. These findings suggest that validity-first automated science systems should be evaluated not only by answer accuracy, but also by whether they indicate when causal claims are unwarranted.

</details>


### [62] [The Dark Room in the Reward Channel: Dense Prediction Rewards Collapse GRPO-Trained LLM Agents -- and What Actually Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21273)
*Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了在 GRPO（组归一化强化学习）框架下，用于长时序稀疏奖励 LLM 代理的密集每步监督（预测下一个观察）会灾难性失效，导致“暗室病理”：预测准确率趋近 100% 但任务成功率降为 0%。核心原因是 GRPO 的标准差归一化在“全失败组”中放大了奖励信号方差，使有界奖励变为无界压力，导致策略崩溃。通过消融实验和方差剖面分析，作者提出了“方差剖面准则”来预测信号是否会被放大器安全处理，并发现奖励通道作用有限，辅助损失通道增益约 20 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集每步监督被视为解决长时序稀疏奖励 LLM 代理问题的有效方案——通过奖励代理预测其下一个观察，从而鼓励记忆形成。然而，该方法在 GRPO 框架下的实际效果尚不清楚。

Method: 在 Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B 模型上于 ALFWorld 任务上评估密集每步监督；使用组归一化强化学习（GRPO）训练；进行单因子消融（移除 GRPO 的 std 归一化）、方差剖面分析、控制信号传递矩阵实验以及打乱金标准安慰剂实验。

Result: 密集每步监督在 GRPO 下导致“暗室病理”：预测准确率 → 1.0，任务成功 → 0，回合长度固定在最大值。移除 GRPO 的 std 归一化可将成功率从 0% 提升至基线水平。方差剖面准则能成功预测此崩溃现象，且与已发表的奖励通道成功案例一致。控制实验表明奖励通道作用中性，辅助损失通道增益约 20 分。

Conclusion: 在 GRPO 框架下，密集每步监督会破坏策略，根本原因在于 GRPO 的归一化放大了全失败组中的奖励信号方差。作者提出的方差剖面准则可用于判断信号是否会被安全处理，该准则不仅解释了已观察到的崩溃，还成功预测了未运行实验臂的结果。

Abstract: Dense per-step supervision is an appealing remedy for sparse-reward, long-horizon LLM agents: reward the agent for predicting its next observation, and memory should follow. We show that under group-normalized RL (GRPO), this recipe does not merely fail -- it destroys the policy. Across Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B on ALFWorld, a potential-based prediction reward drives every run into a degenerate absorbing state (prediction accuracy -> 1.0, task success -> 0,episode length pinned at the horizon): the "dark room" pathology, built automatically by the optimizer. A single-factor ablation localizes the cause -- removing only GRPO's std normalization turns the same reward from catastrophic (0%) into baseline parity -- and a two-line proposition explains why: in all-fail groups the z-scored advantage is invariant to the shaping coefficient, so bounded rewards become unbounded pressure and annealing cannot help. Our central insight generalizes this: what z-scoring amplifies is a dense signal's within-group variance while all-fail groups dominate, so signals whose variance decays by mastery are structurally amplifier-safe.This variance-profile criterion retrodicts our collapses, carries preregistered predictions for arms that had not yet run, and is consistent with published reward-channel successes (a compatibility check, not an independent test). Finally, a controlled signal-delivery matrix (identical signal, varying only the consumption mechanism) shows the reward channel is at best neutral while the auxiliary-loss channel gains ~20 points -- and a shuffled-gold placebo matches the true-gold arm, so the gap survives without correct labels. Endpoints are single-seed; seed replication and group-size controls are preregistered and in progress.

</details>


### [63] [AI Assistants Overassist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21306)
*Verona Teo,Raghav Jain,Tobias Gerstenberg,Max Kleiman-Weiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as tutors and thought partners, helping users reason through problems. While guidance from AI assistants can scaffold thinking and foster learning, such benefits depend on how they help--for instance, intervening too early or too frequently may hinder true learning and cognitive engagement. Yet how AI systems navigate intervention decisions during problem-solving remains poorly understood. Here, we introduce Int-Bench, a simulation-based benchmark for evaluating LLM interventions during learning. Int-Bench simulates a "student" solving a problem while a "teacher" monitors the student's reasoning and decides whether, when, and how to intervene. Across three domains--code debugging, mathematics, and brain teasers--we evaluate LLM teachers on the frequency and timing of interventions, as well as their impact on both immediate task success and generalization to new problems. We also compare LLMs to humans, finding that LLMs intervene more frequently and earlier than humans. Moreover, in contrast to humans, they tend to provide complete solutions rather than targeted hints. These findings suggest that current LLM assistants often optimize for short-term success rather than supporting the reasoning processes needed for deeper learning and long-term success.

</details>


### [64] [Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21453)
*Rajiv Shailesh Chitale,Rahul Madhavan,Taneesh Gupta,Deepanway Ghosal,Aravindan Raghuveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TTEL（Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization）算法，通过token级错误定位提高大语言模型推理效率，在保证性能的同时减少约50%的token生成量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准方法（独立采样、顺序多轮细化）缺乏token级信用分配，导致有效推理前缀被频繁丢弃，计算效率低下。

Method: 利用固定或环境反馈进行token级错误定位：通过比较有信息反馈下的条件概率与空上下文基线，隔离错误发生的步骤，截断轨迹并分支新生成，最大化重用有效前缀。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench上，Qwen3-8B的pass@64达到71.0%，token生成量仅360.4k（约为独立采样的50%）；在AIME-2025和HMMT-2025数学基准上，Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507均优于竞争基线。

Conclusion: TTEL建立了严格支配的Pareto前沿，在推理质量与计算效率之间取得最佳平衡，显著提升了大语言模型在复杂推理和编程任务上的表现。

Abstract: Scaling inference-time computation has emerged as a reliable method to improve the performance of large language models on complex reasoning and programming tasks. However, standard approaches such as independent sampling and sequential multi-turn refinement operate without token-level credit assignment, resulting in computational inefficiency, since valid reasoning prefixes are frequently discarded. In this work, we introduce Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization (TTEL), an inference-time algorithm that utilizes fixed or environment feedback to perform token-level error localization. By comparing conditional probabilities under informed feedback against a null-context baseline, TTEL isolates the step at which an error occurred. The algorithm then truncates the trajectory and branches a new generation, maximally reusing the valid prefix. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TTEL establishes strictly dominating Pareto frontiers across sequential reasoning domains, measured by pass-at-k vs. generated-token cost. With Qwen3-8B on LiveCodeBench, TTEL attains a pass@64 of 71.0% while generating approximately half as many tokens as independent sampling (360.4k vs. 735.0k). Generalizing to math benchmarks AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025, TTEL cleanly outperforms competing test-time baselines across both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507.

</details>


### [65] [Windowed-MTP: Removing the Full-Context Draft-KV Tax at Million-Token Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21535)
*Alagappan Valliappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对百万级上下文中原生MTP投机解码头因全注意力机制导致成本剧增的问题，提出Windowed-MTP训练无关的滑动窗口方案，将草稿KV缓存大小限制为常数级，使解码成本降低28%-44%，同时保持目标模型验证输出分布不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型上下文长度扩展至百万级，原生MTP/NEXTN草稿头在每个草稿步都对整个KV缓存执行全注意力机制，其读取成本与上下文长度线性增长并成为主导，在投机解码最需要加速的百万级上下文场景下反而可能变成净负收益。

Method: 采用StreamingLLM风格的滑动窗口加注意力汇（attention sink）仅应用于草稿头的注意力机制，保留全注意力验证器不变，实现训练无关、即插即用且无损的设计；通过紧凑环形缓冲区回收未读草稿KV。

Result: 在1M上下文下，Windowed-MTP将草稿KV工作集减少约99%，单GPU单模型上每解码步成本比原生MTP降低28%-44%，输入不变性边际随上下文长度扩大而增加，接受率提升时端到端解码延迟同比例改善，且保持目标模型的验证输出分布不变。

Conclusion: Windowed-MTP证明了在投机解码中仅对草稿头应用上下文窗口策略即可显著降低KV开销，无需重新训练目标模型，为扩展到百万级上下文的原生MTP解码提供了高效的工程解决方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by having a cheap draft propose tokens that a target verifies in parallel. Frontier models increasingly ship a built-in Multi-Token-Prediction (MTP/NEXTN) draft head under the assumption that the draft is negligibly cheap. At million-token context this breaks: an MTP draft head typically runs full attention over the entire KV cache at every draft step, so its read grows linearly with context and comes to dominate the draft cost -- precisely where speculation is most valuable. The effect compounds with draft length (a deep native draft can turn net-negative, slower than no speculation) and sharpens under hybrid/linear-attention targets, where cheaper verification leaves the draft's full-attention read exposed. We apply a StreamingLLM-style sliding window plus attention sink to the draft's attention only (Windowed-MTP), leaving full-attention verification intact. It is training-free, drop-in, and lossless by construction: the full-attention target still decides every accepted token, so windowing changes only which tokens are proposed, never which are accepted. It bounds the draft's KV working set to a constant, dropping ~99% of KV entries at 1M. Across three architecture families (Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B and a Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B) at 1M context on a single GPU in SGLang, windowing cuts the per-decode-step cost over the shipping native MTP draft by +28% to +44%, an input-invariant margin that widens with context. Since per-token latency is this cost divided by acceptance length, at matched acceptance end-to-end decode latency improves by the same amount, and more where windowing also lifts acceptance, while preserving the target's verified output distribution. Finally, the unread draft KV -- 7.7-11% of total KV at 1M -- is reclaimed via a compact ring buffer at no acceptance or quality cost.

</details>


### [66] [X$^3$-OPD: Distilling Reasoning into Large Audio-Language Models via On-Policy Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21550)
*Dongjie Fu,Di Cao,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Wenxu Jia,Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Yu Zhang,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: X³-OPD 是一种跨模态在线策略蒸馏框架，通过从强大的文本教师模型向音频语言学生模型转移推理能力，显著提升了音频基础推理和思维链质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型在深度逻辑推理方面仍落后于基于文本的大语言模型，主要原因是高质量音频推理数据的稀缺。

Method: 1. 跨模态在线策略蒸馏框架；2. 学生模型基于自身听觉感知生成推理轨迹；3. 教师模型使用匹配的文本输入和验证答案提供代币级指导；4. 构建三级对称语料库：文本推理转换为语音、基于复杂声学场景的音频事件推理、包含副语言线索的对话推理。

Result: 在MMSU、MMAU、BIG Bench Audio和MMAR数据集上的实验表明，X³-OPD显著提升了音频基础推理和思维链质量，同时很大程度上保留了模型在域偏移下的现有能力。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将跨模态蒸馏从可恢复的文本内容扩展到基于非语言事件、韵律和对话语境的推理，有效弥合了音频语言模型与基于文本模型在推理能力上的差距。

Abstract: While large audio-language models have achieved remarkable progress in auditory perception, they still lag behind text-based large language models in deep logical reasoning, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality audio reasoning data. To bridge this gap, we propose X$^3$-OPD, a cross-modal on-policy distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from a powerful text teacher to an audio-language student. During training, the student generates reasoning trajectories conditioned on its own acoustic perception, while the teacher provides token-level guidance using matched textual inputs and verified answers. We further construct a three-tier symmetric corpus covering textual reasoning rendered into speech, audio-event reasoning grounded in complex acoustic scenes, and spoken-dialogue reasoning involving paralinguistic cues. This design extends cross-modal distillation beyond textually recoverable content to reasoning grounded in non-linguistic events, prosody, and conversational context. Experiments on MMSU, MMAU, BIG Bench Audio, and MMAR demonstrate that X$^3$-OPD substantially improves audio-grounded reasoning and chain-of-thought quality while largely preserving the model's existing capabilities under domain shift.

</details>

### [67] [A Real-Time Generalized Nash Equilibrium Framework for Interaction-Aware Autonomous Driving in Mixed Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21043)
*Nouhed Naidja,Mohamed-Cherif Rahal,Steve Pechberti,Stéphane Font,Guillaume Sandou,Marc Revilloud*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Safe and efficient navigation in mixed-traffic environments remains a critical challenge for Autonomous Vehicles (AVs), primarily due to the complex interdependence between the AV's decisions and the unpredictable reactions of human drivers. This paper introduces a comprehensive decision-making framework that formulates the driving interaction as a Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problem (GNEP). Unlike decoupled optimization approaches, this framework explicitly models shared safety and geometric constraints, ensuring that the feasibility of the AV's strategy is dynamically linked to the opponent's actions. To solve this non-convex problem in real-time, we propose a dedicated solver based on Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO). The complete architecture was validated on a test track using a real autonomous Renault Zoé interacting with a human driver. Experimental results demonstrate the system's ability to handle critical scenarios by generating comfortable, human-like trajectories. Benchmarks confirm the solver's operational feasibility, achieving convergence in under 50 ms.

</details>


### [68] [RL-MACRO: A Cybernetic Closed-Loop Intelligence Framework for Multimodal Adaptive Robotic Craniotomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21113)
*Xiao Zhang,Jiaxuan Li,Renzhen Le,Di Wu,Chao Sun,Jiachen Zhu,Haoyuan Zhang,Xiang Li,Jian Liu,Zhenzhi Ying,Pengfei Zhang,Liming Shu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RL-MACRO框架，通过CNN-LSTM观察者和隐式Q学习实现自主机器人开颅手术中的工具-组织交互优化，在牛肋骨和山羊头骨上验证了感知能力和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人开颅手术需要持续调节工具-组织交互以减轻机械过载和热损伤，但由于未知时变组织特性和物理遮挡无法直接测量切割温度，导致过程部分可观测。

Method: RL-MACRO框架包含：1) CNN-LSTM观察者融合力和声音反馈重构隐藏温度状态(R²=0.939)；2) 离线隐式Q学习(IQL)策略；3) 新型双头Actor动态协调进给速度、主轴速度和切割深度；4) 在线轨迹重规划和速度伺服。

Result: 温度重构精度高(R²=0.939, MAE=1.717°C)，在牛肋骨和山羊头骨上验证了系统鲁棒感知、从力/温度偏差中自适应恢复的能力，以及在不规则表面的平滑执行。

Conclusion: 建立了数据驱动的控制论范式，实现了安全高效的自主骨切割，为机器人手术提供了新的自主决策框架。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic craniotomy requires continuous regulation of tool-tissue interactions to mitigate mechanical overload and thermal damage while maintaining surgical efficiency. However, this process is inherently partially observable due to unknown, time-varying tissue properties and the inability to directly measure cutting temperatures under physical occlusion. To address these challenges, we propose RL-MACRO, a cybernetic closed-loop intelligence framework that couples multimodal perception, adaptive decision-making, and robotic execution. This framework empowers the surgical robot to autonomously perceive inaccessible states from partial sensory feedback and dynamically optimize its behaviors under uncertain environment. A CNN-LSTM observer first fuses force and sound feedback to reconstruct the hidden temperature state (R^2=0.939, MAE = 1.717 deg C). This reconstructed temperature, alongside multi-sensor features, forms the belief state for an offline Implicit Q-Learning (IQL) policy. A novel dual-head Actor dynamically coordinates the feed rate, spindle speed, and cutting depth to optimize efficiency within strict safety bounds. These decisions are seamlessly translated into spatial motions via online trajectory re-planning and velocity servoing. Experiments on bovine ribs and six ex vivo goat skulls validate the system's robust perception, adaptive recovery from force/temperature excursions, and smooth execution on irregular surfaces, establishing a data-driven cybernetic paradigm for safe and efficient autonomous bone cutting.

</details>


### [69] [FORGE-plus: Force-Budgeted Recovery for Contact-Rich Assembly with a Frozen LLM Supervisor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21227)
*Kyupaeck Jeff Rah,Midum Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于冻结文本大语言模型(LLM)的两层框架，用于装配任务中的力控制。LLM负责为每个物体分配力上限并从固定动作菜单中选择恢复策略，底层控制器则强制执行该力上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力条件强化学习虽能实现狭小间隙装配，但实际部署需要确定合适的物体力上限，并在插入失败时在不超出上限的情况下恢复。现有方法缺乏针对不同物体的自适应力限制和有效的故障恢复策略。

Method: 采用两层架构：冻结的纯文本LLM在执行前为每个物体分配力上限，并使用紧凑的文本力签名从固定动作菜单中选择恢复机动。底层控制器强制执行力上限，恢复策略不能提高上限，而破坏力阈值仅对评估器可见。框架在刚体模拟环境中运行。

Result: 单一策略在脆弱和鲁棒物体上均通过256/256次评估，正确预测释放时机，平均峰值力5.4 N。在注入抓握滑移情况下，基于力签名的恢复策略分别解决了40%和64%的失败案例，而"用力按压"基线方法要么无效要么导致频繁破坏。PPO在严格力约束下无法解决任务，学习到的释放策略也失败。

Conclusion: 提出的框架成功实现了无需破坏的装配任务，证明了文本LLM在力条件强化学习中的价值。负面结果表明，PPO算法和学习的释放策略在该任务中均不适用，说明力上限分配和恢复策略需要专门设计而非通用学习。实验均在刚体模拟中完成，未进行从仿真到现实的迁移验证。

Abstract: Force-conditioned reinforcement learning (RL) enables tight-clearance assembly under a commanded force ceiling, but practical deployment requires determining an appropriate force limit for each object and recovering from insertion failures without exceeding it. We present a two-layer framework in which a frozen, text-only large language model (LLM) assigns a per-object force ceiling before execution and selects recovery maneuvers from a fixed action menu using compact textual force signatures. The LLM never controls force directly: a low-level controller enforces the force ceiling, the recovery policy cannot increase it, and the hidden breaking-force threshold is known only to the evaluator.
  We evaluate the framework on fragile bottle placement and 0.4 mm diametral-clearance gear insertion using two grippers (Robotiq 2F-140 and Franka Panda hand). A single policy passes 256/256 evaluation episodes on both fragile and robust objects without breakage, correctly predicts release timing, and completes a full table-pick-and-insert pipeline with a mean peak force of 5.4 N. Under injected in-grip slip, the force-signature recovery strategy resolves 40% and 64% of failures on the two grippers, whereas a press-harder baseline is either ineffective or causes frequent breakage. We also report negative results, including the failure of PPO to solve the task under strict force constraints and unsuccessful learned release strategies. All experiments are conducted in rigid-body simulation with hidden force-threshold breakage; no sim-to-real claim is made.

</details>


### [70] [GS-Agent: Creating 4D Physical Worlds With Generative Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21522)
*Hongxin Zhang,Chunru Lin,Junyan Li,Zhou Xian,Tsun-Hsuan Wang,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GS-Agent是一个端到端的多智能体框架，利用物理引擎和基础模型从自然语言描述中生成动态、逼真的4D物理世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算机图形学方法依赖手动创建，需要大量人力；现有生成式基础模型方法在保证物理合理性和可控性方面存在问题。

Method: GS-Agent将任务分解为实体管理（3D资产策划、材料调优、放置、运动控制）和渲染配置（相机和光照操纵）；多个具有不同专业知识的智能体通过代码与物理引擎交互，寻求多模态反馈并协作迭代构建4D世界。

Result: GS-Agent能够将自然语言转换为多样且物理合理的4D世界，展示液体、可变形物体和刚体之间的丰富相互作用，并实现电影级的相机和光照控制。

Conclusion: GS-Agent为4D世界生成的新范式奠定了基础，赋能创意内容创作和物理AI。

Abstract: Creating dynamic and physically realistic 4D worlds from natural language descriptions is both fascinating and challenging. Traditional computer graphics methods rely on manual creation, requiring extensive human effort to fine-tune materials, motions, and visual fidelity. Recent advances in generative foundation models have sparked interest in learning to generate such 4D worlds from large-scale data; however, existing methods still struggle to ensure physical plausibility and controllability. In this work, we take a different path by leveraging foundation models to construct an agentic system that emulates how humans traditionally create 4D worlds, yet automates the entire process. We present GS-Agent, an end-to-end multi-agent framework that integrates physics engines in the loop to generate realistic, dynamic, and controllable 4D physical worlds from natural language. Inspired by how humans build 4D worlds, GS-Agent decomposes the task into entity management, covering 3D asset curation, material tuning, placement, and motion control, and rendering configuration, including camera and lighting manipulation. Multiple agents with distinct expertise interact with the physics engine via code, seek multimodal feedback, and collaborate to iteratively construct 4D worlds that align with the given descriptions. Experimental results show that GS-Agent effectively converts natural language into diverse and physically plausible 4D worlds exhibiting rich interactions among liquids, deformable objects, and rigid bodies, while achieving cinematic camera and lighting control. We envision GS-Agent as a foundation for a new paradigm in 4D world generation, empowering creative content creation and physical AI. Project page at https://umass-embodied-agi.github.io/gs-agent/

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [71] [AINTMA: Agentic AI Architecture for Autonomous Test Management with Generative Intelligence, Secure Cloud Communication and Adaptive Quality Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20452)
*Vinil Pasupuleti,Shyalendar Reddy Allala,Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu,Shrey Tyagi,Srinivasateja Songa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Modern software quality assurance demands intelligent, autonomous systems capable of adaptive decision-making across distributed cloud environments. This paper presents AINTMA (Agentic Intelligent Test Management Architecture), a multi-agent agentic AI system that transforms traditional test management into an autonomous quality intelligence ecosystem. AINTMA deploys six specialized AI agents (Test Discovery, Risk Assessment, Reinforcement Learning Prioritization, Execution Orchestration, Generative Quality Intelligence, and Cloud Security Monitor) coordinated through a secure multi-agent communication framework over a cloud-native microservices infrastructure. The Generative Quality Intelligence agent employs large language models to produce plain language quality narratives, defect risk summaries, and data-augmented test recommendations. The RL Prioritization agent models test selection as a Markov Decision Process, learning contextual policies from large-scale historical test execution data (47 features, rolling 36-month window). Secure cloud communication is enforced through a zero-trust API gateway with OAuth2/JWT authentication, encrypted inter-agent messaging, and multi-tenant isolation. Evaluation across 12 heterogeneous software projects over 18 months demonstrates: 88.4% test prioritization accuracy (APFD, vs. 51.2% random, 82.1% best commercial baseline); 43% test cycle time reduction; defect escape rate reduced from 8.3% to 2.1%; 340% ROI at 9-month payback. The agentic architecture scales to 50,000+ test cases with sub-400ms response time, and the generative intelligence module achieves 4.3/5.0 developer usefulness rating. AINTMA demonstrates that agentic AI, combining autonomous multi-agent coordination, generative intelligence and secure smart connectivity, can fundamentally advance software quality management in cloud-scale enterprise environments.

</details>


### [72] [Marking the Wrong Symptoms: Evaluating LLM Watermarks in Medical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20462)
*Melanie Rieff,Robin Staab,Thibaud Gloaguen,Stefan Hegselmann,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统评估了LLM水印在医疗领域的表现，发现水印技术会显著降低医疗模型性能，导致词汇损坏、术语幻觉和图像发现错误归因等问题，强调了医疗领域专用评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型越来越多地集成到临床工作流程中，需要可靠的模型输出生成可追溯性（水印）。然而，大多数水印评估仅使用通用基准，导致医疗等特殊领域（其中微小的token级扰动可能导致显著的语义变化）被忽视。

Method: 在11个LLM和7个VLM上测试5种水印方案，涵盖单模态和多模态临床推理任务。引入由人类专家验证的系统性审计流程，评估医疗推理质量、术语精确性和诱导的幻觉。

Result: 水印在多种失败模式下导致性能显著下降，包括词汇损坏、术语幻觉、图像发现错误归因或遗漏。缺乏领域特定分析和汇总指标会系统性地掩盖水印引起的问题。

Conclusion: 医疗领域专用评估是安全部署水印模型的先决条件，因为当前基准可能会掩盖临床相关的失败。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical workflows, stressing the need for reliable traceability of model-generated output with watermarking. Yet, most watermarks are evaluated on general-purpose benchmarks, leaving domains like medicine, where small token-level perturbations can result in significant semantic changes, underexplored. In this work, we present the first rigorous study of how LLM watermarks affect medical performance, benchmarking 5 watermarking schemes across 11 LLMs and 7 VLMs on various tasks spanning unimodal and multimodal clinical reasoning. Importantly, we complement existing evaluations by introducing a human-expert-validated pipeline for systematically auditing medical reasoning quality, terminological precision, and induced hallucinations. Our results reveal that watermarking can induce substantial degradation across multiple failure modes, including lexical corruption, hallucinated terminology, and amplified misattribution or omission of image findings. Notably, we find that the absence of domain-specific analyses, combined with aggregate metrics that miss failures inherent to clinical text, can systematically obscure practical watermark-induced degradations. Our findings establish domain-specific evaluation as a prerequisite for the safe deployment of watermarked models in medicine, where current benchmarks can otherwise mask clinically consequential failures.

</details>


### [73] [Stochastic Sampling is Epistemically Shallow: The Dimensionality Gap Between Temperature Variation and Model Diversity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20464)
*Izhar Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了自洽性（自投票）与多样化LLM集成在不确定性估计方面的效果。研究表明，自洽性能提供准确的单题不确定性估计，但无法揭示跨题结构；只有多样化的集成模型能检测出模型未掌握的知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM重复运行中的回答变化是否能揭示模型未掌握的知识，比较自洽性（自投票）与多样化集成在不确定性估计方面的差异。

Method: 在相同问题上比较两种设置：单模型运行100次（τ=1）与24个LLM的集成运行一次（τ=0）。使用Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵检验将信号与采样噪声分离，在五个数据集家族和三个基准测试（MMLU、HellaSwag、GSM8K）上进行评估。

Result: 在单模型内，五个数据集家族中最多只有一个维度超过噪声；在集成中，四个特征值超过噪声阈值；而在匹配难度的伯努利零假设模型中，500次蒙特卡洛抽样中最多只有一个特征值超过噪声。

Conclusion: 自洽性能够提供准确的单题不确定性估计，但无法检测出跨题结构；只有多样化的集成模型能揭示模型未掌握的知识。

Abstract: When a language model gives different answers on repeated runs, does that variation reveal what it does not know? Self-consistency turns the variation into a per-question uncertainty estimate via majority voting. But does the same variation reveal cross-question structure -- related questions flipping together, the way a diverse ensemble does? We compare two regimes on the same questions: one model run $100$ times at $τ=1$ versus an ensemble of $24$ LLMs run once each at $τ=0$. A Marchenko--Pastur random-matrix test separates signal from sampling noise on both sides. Within any single model, at most one dimension rises above noise across five families and three benchmarks (MMLU, HellaSwag, GSM8K). Across the ensemble, four eigenvalues clear the noise edge, while a matched-difficulty Bernoulli null produces at most one in $500$ Monte Carlo draws. Self-consistency gives accurate per-question uncertainty but no detectable cross-question structure; only a diverse ensemble surfaces what a model does not know.

</details>


### [74] [DC-Leap: Training-Free Acceleration of dLLMs via Draft-Guided Contiguous Leaping Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20467)
*Yanhua Jiao,Tianyi Wu,Xiaoxi Sun,Yulin Li,HuiLing Zhen,Libo Qin,Baotian Hu,Zhuotao Tian,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DC-Leap 是一种无需训练的 dLLM 加速框架，通过动态连续验证和草稿引导解码策略克服 JPDE 错误，在保持生成质量的同时实现高达 53.19x（MBPP 长序列）甚至 105.02x（结合 KV-Cache）的显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 dLLM 并行解码策略因 JPDE（联合概率依赖误差）而依赖过于保守的置信度阈值，导致冗余去噪迭代和推理速度低下。

Method: 引入动态连续验证策略，将严格有序的因果约束整合到并行解码中，逐步验证 token 依赖；结合草稿引导解码机制，在保持双向注意力结构优势的同时实现多 token 跨越。

Result: 在标准基准上，DC-Leap 在 MBPP 长序列生成上实现最高 53.19x 加速，与 KV-Cache 结合时可达 105.02x 加速，且生成质量相当。

Conclusion: DC-Leap 有效中和了 JPDE，在中等置信度区间实现可靠加速，显著优于现有策略，代码已开源。

Abstract: While parallel decoding is central to the efficiency of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), current strategies are often hindered by overly conservative confidence thresholds. These thresholds, necessitated by the Joint Probability Dependence Error (JPDE), result in redundant denoising iterations and suboptimal inference speeds. To overcome this, we propose DC-Leap, a training-free framework that enables reliable acceleration of dLLMs in the moderate-confidence regime. DC-Leap introduces a Dynamic Contiguous Verification strategy that integrates strictly-ordered causal constraints into the parallel decoding process. By progressively validating token dependencies, this mechanism effectively neutralizes the JPDE, enabling reliable acceleration with comparable performance. Furthermore, DC-Leap incorporates the draft-guided decoding mechanism, where the draft helps extend the context by leaping forward across multiple tokens, providing look-ahead context and retaining the structural benefits of bidirectional attention during inference. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DC-Leap achieves substantial speedups, up to 53.19x on MBPP for long-sequence generation, and up to 105.02x when combined with KV-Cache with comparable generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/ffh-wyls/DC-Leap .

</details>


### [75] [InferenceBench: A Benchmark for Open-Ended LLM Inference Optimization by AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20468)
*Jehyeok Yeon,Ben Rank,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InferenceBench is an open-ended benchmark for evaluating AI agents' ability to optimize LLM inference performance through deploying inference servers, revealing that agents struggle with diverse configuration exploration despite having domain knowledge.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing AI agent benchmarks evaluate on prescribed workflows with narrow action spaces, where tasks can be solved by memorizing known solutions rather than genuine optimization, making it unclear whether strong results reflect true capability.

Method: Agents deploy an OpenAI-compatible inference server to optimize LLM inference speed, receiving a target LLM, one H100 GPU, an optimization scenario, and a 2-hour wall-clock budget. Four scenarios isolate prefill latency, decode latency, concurrent request throughput, and a balanced scenario.

Result: Across 15 frontier agent configurations, agents improved over naive PyTorch baseline (up to $8.08\times$) and matched default serving engines (vLLM $4.05\times$), but fell below simple hyperparameter search (up to $11.53\times$). Qualitative analysis showed agents converged on a single inference framework, testing few distinct configurations and spending most budget re-measuring/optimizing rather than exploring new strategies.

Conclusion: The bottleneck for agents is not domain knowledge but the ability to propose diverse configurations, evaluate them systematically, and submit the best solution. InferenceBench effectively reflects agents' ability to operate in open-ended AI engineering settings where memorized solutions lead to limited improvements.

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used to automate research and development tasks, yet existing benchmarks typically evaluate them on prescribed workflows or narrow action spaces. Even nominally open-ended tasks can often be solved by retrieving a well-known recipe and tuning a few hyperparameters, making it unclear whether strong results reflect genuine optimization or memorized solutions. We introduce InferenceBench, where an agent must deploy an OpenAI-compatible inference server and optimize the speed of LLM inference. Each agent receives a target LLM, one H100 GPU, an optimization scenario, and a wall-clock time budget of two hours. Three optimization scenarios isolate distinct bottlenecks of inference (prefill latency, decode latency, and concurrent request throughput) and a fourth balances all three at the same time. Across 15 frontier agent configurations, agents reliably improve over a naive PyTorch baseline (up to $8.08\times$) and often match or exceed serving engines with default settings ($4.05\times$ for vLLM), but still fall below a simple hyperparameter search under the same time budget (up to $11.53\times$). Qualitative analysis of agent trajectories shows that although agents enumerate many relevant optimization techniques, they overwhelmingly converge on a single inference framework. They test only a few distinct configurations and spend the remaining budget re-measuring, repairing, or optimizing hyperparameters rather than exploring substantially different strategies. This suggests the bottleneck is not domain knowledge, but the ability to propose diverse configurations, evaluate them systematically, and submit the best identified solution. Overall, InferenceBench reflects the ability of agents to operate in an open-ended AI engineering setting, where memorized solutions lead to limited improvements.

</details>


### [76] [DecodeShare: Tracing the Shared Subspace of LLM Decode-Time Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20469)
*Zishan Shao,Lixun Zhang,Kangning Cui,Yixiao Wang,Ting Jiang,Hancheng Ye,Qinsi Wang,Zhixu Du,Yuzhe Fu,Fan Yang,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen,Hai Helen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DecodeShare协议，在LLM解码时间识别出跨任务共享的低维子空间，并证明该子空间对模型性能和激活引导具有重要因果作用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在KV缓存推理下，LLM使用单一参数集处理多种任务，但解码时究竟使用了何种任务通用结构尚不明确，且现有方法主要关注prefill阶段的解释，解码阶段缺乏有效分析

Method: 提出DecodeShare协议：1) 识别解码时间隐藏状态中跨任务一致共享的低维子空间；2) 通过仅在解码时移除该子空间来测试其因果作用

Result: 实验表明，移除发现的共享子空间比移除prefill衍生或随机子空间对性能的负面影响更大；解码共享子空间与常见引导方向重叠，投影该子空间可分离不同组件的功能角色

Conclusion: 解码共享子空间可作为解码时间的高杠杆因果通道；直接投影该子空间可分离功能角色；在解码时评估引导向量比基于prefill的代理能提供更可靠的部署信号

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) handle many tasks with one set of parameters, but under KV-cached inference it is unclear what task-general structure, if any, is used at decode time rather than during prefill. We propose DecodeShare, a protocol that identifies a low-dimensional subspace consistently shared across tasks in decode-time hidden states, and then tests its causal role by removing that subspace only during decoding. In our experiments, disturbing the discovered shared subspace degrades decision performance far more than disturbing either a prefill-derived or random subspace under the same intervention budget. We further show this decode-shared subspace has practical consequences for activation steering: common steering directions can overlap the task-general decode channel. Projecting out this shared subspace directly separates the functional roles of the two components, while evaluating steering vectors at decode-time yields more reliable signal for downstream deployment than prefill-based proxies. Despite its compactness, the shared subspace can serve as a high-leverage causal channel at decode time. Code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/decodeshare.git.

</details>


### [77] [PlanE: Meta Planning of Data, Tuning, and Inference for Extractive-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20470)
*Jiacheng Wang,Weiyan Zhang,Guangya Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlanE是一个用于构建任务特定LLM的规划框架，通过数据分解、指令微调和提示推理来解决标注成本高和缺乏优化方法的问题，同时引入DTI规划器来选择最佳的基础LLM和组合以提高构建效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强LLM的任务特定能力主要需要大量指令微调数据集，但数据量巨大导致标注成本高，且缺乏针对特定任务的优化方法。

Method: PlanE框架包含数据分解、指令微调和提示推理；引入Data-Tuning-Inference (DTI)规划器，用于选择特定数据集的最佳基础LLM及其DTI组合以提高构建效率。

Result: 实验结果表明PlanE在两种视角下均有效：相同基础LLM跨不同数据集，以及不同基础LLM在相同数据集上；同时验证了DTI规划器在不同优化目标下的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 代码已开源（https://github.com/gugugu-469/PlanE），该框架为解决LLM任务特定能力构建中的标注成本和优化问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Enhancing the task-specific capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) primarily requires substantial instruction-tuning datasets. However, the sheer volume of such data imposes a considerable annotation cost, and a lack of optimization methods for tailoring LLMs to specific tasks. To address the above issues, we propose a \textbf{Plan}ning framework for constructing \textbf{E}xtractive-based LLMs called \textbf{PlanE}, which includes data decomposition, instruction tuning, and prompt inference. Additionally, we introduce a Data-Tuning-Inference (DTI) planner, aimed at selecting the optimal base-LLM and its DTI combinations for specific datasets to improve construction efficiency. The experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our PlanE from two views: (1) across different datasets using the same base-LLM, and (2) on the same dataset using different base-LLMs. Furthermore, we validate the generalizability of the proposed DTI planner under different optimization objectives. The codes are publicly available at https://github.com/gugugu-469/PlanE.

</details>


### [78] [Robust Critics: Defending LLMs Against Multi-Turn Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20472)
*Roman Belaire,Arunesh Sinha,Pradeep Varakantham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DCGS通过在每个对话轮次推断用户意图，利用基于价值和后悔的评判器区分有害攻击与善意提问，提升多轮对话中的LLM安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮对话中，攻击者的真实意图往往逐渐显露，但现有安全框架将其视为上下文赌博机问题，忽略了对话轨迹。区分恶意攻击与善意提问的模糊性是LLM安全的核心挑战：假设最坏会伤害合法用户，假设最好则容易被利用。

Method: DCGS将对抗性对话建模为马尔可夫决策过程，在token级和 utterance 级学习基于价值和后悔的评判器，通过动作价值评判器对候选响应进行评分，利用推理时重加权近似基础策略的指数倾斜，而非应用固定安全规则。

Result: 在 CARES-18k、WildJailbreak、Redbench 和 Harmbench 等数据集上，DCGS 优于强鲁棒基线和前沿模型；此外，DCGS 可迁移至前沿模型，无需微调即可提升其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DCGS 有效解决了多轮对话中意图模糊的挑战，通过利用对话历史推断用户意图，提供了一种优于现有方法的原则性安全框架。

Abstract: When a user asks a language model something harmful, is it a genuine attack or a misunderstood but well-meaning question? This ambiguity is one of the central challenges of LLM safety. A model that assumes the worst harms legitimate users; one that assumes the best is easily exploited. The problem is compounded in multi-turn dialogue, where an attacker's true intent may only reveal itself gradually across many exchanges, yet existing safety frameworks apply a contextual bandit treatment, ignoring the trajectory of the conversation.
  To that end, we propose Dialogue Critic Guided Sampling (DCGS), a framework that addresses this by inferring user intent at every turn of dialogue. Instead of applying a fixed rule about what is or is not safe, DCGS learns what the user's intent is likely to be based on the full conversational history and generates responses accordingly. Formally, we model adversarial dialogue as a Markov Decision Process and learn value and regret-based critics at both the individual token and utterance (full response) levels, scoring candidate responses via an action-value critic. We prove that this inference-time reweighting approximates exponential tilting of the base policy, guaranteeing improvement in expected return for any finite candidate pool, a property that group-relative objectives do not exhibit. Evaluated on CARES-18k, WildJailbreak, Redbench, and Harmbench, DCGS outperforms strong robust baselines and frontier models on adversarial dialogue tasks. DCGS also transfers to frontier models, improving their robustness without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [79] [SonicSampler: Unified Tile-Aware Kernels for LLM Sampling and Speculative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20475)
*Pragaash Ponnusamy,Shivam Sahni,Jue Wang,Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SonicSampler 是一个用于 LLM 推理采样加速的统一 Triton 内核套件，通过垂直融合采样流水线实现单内核批量执行，并提出两级分层 top-k 算法，在异构推测解码工作负载上达到最高 16 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推理采样实现存在三方面局限：仅加速流水线子集、依赖多次内核启动、假设批量内采样行为同质化，导致无法支持动态服务负载且无法高效利用 CUDA Graph 执行。

Method: 提出 SonicSampler，采用 tile-aware Triton 内核垂直融合完整采样流水线，设计可 workload-aware 的固定执行模型；核心是两级分层 top-k 算法，利用 LLM 输出的低熵结构实现高效词汇表选择，支持动态每请求采样行为（语法约束、重复/频率/存在惩罚、logit 偏置、温度缩放、top-k/p/min-p 过滤、推测验证）并保持单内核批量执行与完全 CUDA Graph 兼容。

Result: 在竞争基线中，两级分层 top-k 算法实现最高 10 倍加速；在异构推测解码工作负载上，SonicSampler 相较最先进基线实现最高 16 倍加速，同时保持灵活批量执行。

Conclusion: SonicSampler 通过统一采样流水线融合与高效分层 top-k 算法，有效突破现有实现的性能瓶颈，支持动态服务负载与 CUDA Graph 执行，显著提升 LLM 推理吞吐。

Abstract: Sampling in LLM inference comprises a combinatorial set of logit processing, token selection, and verification operations for speculative decoding. However, existing implementations either accelerate only subsets of this pipeline, rely on multiple kernel launches, or assume homogeneous sampling behavior across a batch, limiting support for dynamic serving workloads and preventing efficient CUDA Graph execution. We present $\textbf{SonicSampler}$, a unified suite of tile-aware Triton kernels that vertically fuses the complete sampling pipeline into a fixed, workload-aware execution model. Our kernels support dynamic per-request sampling behaviors, including grammar-constrained decoding, repetition, frequency and presence penalties, logit bias, temperature scaling, top-$k$ / top-$p$ / min-$p$ filtering, and speculative verification - within a single batched kernel while remaining fully CUDA Graph-compatible. Central to our approach is a novel hierarchical two-stage top-$k$ algorithm that achieves up to $\textbf{10x speedup}$ over competitive baselines and exploits the low-entropy structure of LLM outputs to enable efficient selection over large vocabularies. Across heterogeneous speculative decoding workloads, SonicSampler achieves up to $\textbf{16x speedup}$ over state-of-the-art baselines while preserving flexible batched execution.

</details>


### [80] [Semi-Supervised Text-Attributed Graph Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20477)
*Yurui Lai,Samir Moustafa,Renchi Yang,Tsz Nam Chan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于Wasserstein距离的TAGs半监督学习框架，通过图-文本协作编码和LLM文本合成模块，实现高效的TAG学习与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TAGs表示学习方法存在可扩展性问题，数据蒸馏方法无法捕捉模态间复杂相互作用、难以处理半监督标签稀缺问题，且缺乏生成下游LLM任务所需的人类可读文本属性的能力。

Method: 1. 提出以Wasserstein距离（WSD）为指导的统一半监督框架；2. 引入图-文本协作编码模块，采用双路径编码器（图感知和图无关）在协作自训练方案中工作；3. 开发WSD-based图草图算法；4. 开发成本效益高的LLM文本合成模块，利用基于聚类的关键词提取生成节点摘要。

Result: 在基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在GNN和LLM下游任务中均实现了最先进性能-压缩权衡，能够有效且高效地进行TAG学习或分析。

Conclusion: \algo{}框架成功解决了现有方法在可扩展性、模态交互捕捉、标签稀缺处理和文本属性生成等方面的局限，为TAGs的高效学习与分析提供了新解决方案。

Abstract: {\em Text-Attributed Graphs} (TAGs) have emerged as an expressive data model for integrating graph topology with rich textual semantics. Existing representation learning methods over TAGs suffer from severe scalability bottlenecks, particularly together with {\em Large Language Models} (LLMs). While data distillation offers a promising data-centric solution, existing methods fail to capture the complex interplay between graph and text modalities, struggle with the label scarcity inherent in semi-supervised settings, and lack the ability to produce the human-readable textual attributes required for downstream LLM-based tasks.
  To address these challenges, we propose \algo{}, a unified semi-supervised framework guided by the {\em Wasserstein Distance} (WSD). Grounded in our empirical findings on real TAGs, \algo{} introduces a graph-text collaborative encoding module that utilizes dual-pathway encoders (graph-aware and -free) within a collaborative self-training scheme to harvest reliable pseudo-labels and fuse complementary graph-text features. Furthermore, we develop a theoretically grounded WSD-based graph sketching algorithm and a cost-effective LLM text synthesis module, which leverages cluster-based keyword extraction to generate coherent, human-readable summaries for condensed nodes. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that \algo{} achieves a state-of-the-art performance-compression trade-off in terms of both GNN- and LLM-based downstream tasks, enabling effective and efficient TAG learning or analytics.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond Liars' Bench: The Impact of Lie Typology, Depth, and Sparsity on Deception Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20479)
*Amr Moustafa,Max Feser,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统性分析了影响大型语言模型欺骗输出检测性能的关键因素（表示深度、探针表达能力、稀疏特征表示、欺骗类型），发现欺骗检测是一个高度依赖表示的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测方法在跨域场景下效果不佳，训练一种欺骗类型的数据难以泛化到其他欺骗类型，需要系统性研究各种因素对检测性能的影响。

Method: 在标准基准训练数据基础上，补充包含多样化欺骗类型（伪造、省略、夸大）的数据集，分析表示深度、探针表达能力、稀疏特征表示和欺骗类型学七个探针类型的影响。

Result: 最优表示深度高度依赖数据集，更表达能力的探针只有选择性增益，稀疏自编码器特征与密集隐藏状态表现相似，训练数据和欺骗类型的选择显著改变可检测性。

Conclusion: 欺骗检测是一个高度表示依赖的问题，检测性能受训练数据和欺骗类型学影响显著。

Abstract: Training probes to detect deceptive outputs from large language models is still an open problem. Recent work has demonstrated that detection probes fail especially in out-of-domain scenarios -- training on one type of lie does not transfer well to deception scenarios involving other types of lies. In this work, we conduct a systematic study on how various factors impact detection performance: representation depth, probe expressivity, sparse feature representations, and the lie typology of the training data. To this end, we augment standard benchmark training data with a supplementary dataset containing diverse types of deception, including fabrication, omission, and exaggeration examples. Analyzing these factors across seven probe types, our experimental results show that the optimal representation depth is highly dataset-dependent, more expressive probes provide only selective gains over linear baselines, and sparse autoencoder features perform similarly to dense hidden states. Ultimately, we demonstrate that the choice of training data and lie typology substantially changes detectability, highlighting that deception detection is a highly representation-dependent problem.

</details>


### [82] [Routing Without Training: Controllable-Ratio LLM Offloading via Reliability Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20481)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Kevin S Chan,Su Wang,Christopher Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的本地-云端协作框架 CARGO，通过分析本地模型在推理时不同采样响应的一致性来决定何时信任本地执行，何时将任务卸载到云端更强的模型，在多种任务和模型上超越现有无训练基线，甚至在某些场景下优于监督学习的路由器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有本地-云端协作方法依赖训练好的路由器或协作感知微调，但这些方法将路由行为绑定到特定运行模式。作者认为这种训练可能是不必要的，因为本地模型自身推理时采样响应的一致性已提供足够强的信号来指导协作决策。
